# Final report: real06

34 views: 0 polished, 34 Cycles (gate_validation 6, not_run 28). Stages: render run, gate validation not_run, polish not_run, vision check single_pass (calibration run). A polished image is final only when the gate accepted it and the vision check checked it without finding a lost or added element (§5.5). Mismatches are listed with their evidence and never auto-fixed.

## AI decor

29 decor items chosen by the AI (Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8; both passes agreeing) in 9 rooms; 5 items by the rules. The decor stage places decor only: it moves, adds and removes no furniture (the AI completion of furnished rooms has its own section, `AI completion of furnished rooms`; `furniture/<project>/decor_report.md` has every room).

U: unverified pieces in view.
